## 2026-10-27 — Body Configuration merged actions and safe migration

- **Baseline / implemented:** clean main `b640c7d` had 24 selectable conditions and 21 action entries, including finite Forward/Back Move, separate movement/aim/jump/dodge choices and Hold actions. Consolidated to 21 conditions / 10 parameterized action entries. Move uses fixed Left/Right, Toward Enemy, Away from Enemy or Arena Center while all conditions remain live, releasing to existing lower/manual fallback on loss. Removed its distance endpoint, completion event, completion lock and facing lock. Keep Distance renames Short/Mid/Long to Close/Mid/Far with unchanged bands/hysteresis. Auto Aim, Dodge and Jump reuse their existing parameter branches; J04 becomes Change Platform, F02 becomes Short Burst. Removed A25/A56/A61 and M10/F06 from selection/validation. Other predicates, resources, timing, commitments and priorities remain unchanged.
- **Compatibility / files:** ConditionalLoadout owns explicit idempotent action migration, typed parameters, validation and full parameter labels for setup/Help/F8/HUD/analytics snapshots. Equivalent M01/M02/M09/Q02/D02–D04/J03 rows map to their matching merged branch. M04 maps to unspecified Move direction and cannot run until reselected; it never maps Forward/Back to Left/Right. Deleted conditions stay in their original slots and invalidate the entire rule, including the extra-condition tail. Deleted actions stay invalid without a substitute. Copies retain unrelated fields/order. Targeted controller/UI/preset updates and mirrored authority/current docs are included; no serialized scene/prefab, ProjectSettings, package, tuning or Legacy edits.
- **Compile / happy path / core flow:** normal Unity compilation passed. Final bounded Editor checks: **109 PASS / 0 FAIL** in `Logs/BossEncounter/configuration-actions.txt`, plus **21 PASS / 0 FAIL** in `configuration-action-edges.txt`. Covers all parameter buttons and preview/JSON/cancel copies, five physical Move branches beyond the old 1.5-unit limit, condition gain/loss/manual fallback, lower-row priority, extra-condition loss, event expiry, unchanged distance bands, both Auto Aim target strategies, Jump Once/Repeat, all four physical Dodge directions, Jump priority, Higher/Lower transfers, center arrival, aligned-target stopping/resumption, target loss, Boss collision, committed six-shot Short Burst, invalid Start Fight, six valid ordered independent presets, exact final preset parameters, full HUD branch labels, pause/configuration cleanup and advancing W1/W2/W3. Real preset unlock progress and input devices restored; returned to Edit Mode. `git diff --check` passed; scene clean with no missing scripts.
- **Actual captures:** inspected `Logs/BossEncounter/rule-cards-merged-M11-options.png`, `rule-cards-merged-D01-options.png` (1280x720) and `rule-cards-merged-combat-rules.png` (2560x1440). Parameters fit the option row and remain present in configuration/preview/combat cards.
- **Known issue outside this change:** after exiting Play Mode, Console reported four TMP_FontAsset OnDestroy errors refusing to destroy asset-backed atlas/material references. The character font clone/disposal path in DuelUI.CharacterCreation is unchanged by this task; no font assets were edited. This occurred after the completed gameplay checks and is recorded rather than expanding this action/catalog task into font cleanup.
- **Tooling / limits:** CLI update check hit ERR_NETWORK; sandbox discovery could not read the Editor descriptor. The existing Unity connection verified the correct project and ran compilation/checks. One temporary tool script needed a fully qualified UnityEditor.Editor type. ## 2026-10-22 — DisplayOrder arbitration for conflicting Rules

- **Baseline / cause:** fetched `origin/main`; clean main at `0615fc2`. The runtime selected operation-array rows independently, while the physical executor always tried Dodge before Jump. Thus simultaneous Rules could compete for the same axes, displayed order could disagree with runtime order, and a later Dodge could erase an accepted Jump's vertical velocity. `BossEncounterSmoke.RunRuleConflicts()` reproduced **3 failures** (reordered opposite Move, Jump-first selection and Jump completion protection) before the fix.
- **Change:** `ConditionalController` caches the existing `OrderedRules()` traversal while preserving operation-local row identities. Every row is still observed; existing continuation, executable fallback and consumption logic now select winners in global DisplayOrder. Move and Jump can run in parallel; Dodge and platform transfer each require both physical controls. Accepted automatic Jump owns vertical control through actual landing; existing Dodge/platform/Burst commitments take precedence over ordinary rematching. The platform partial now uses this shared arbitration instead of its separate Move-priority check. Inspected `PlayerInputReader` and `PlayerMovement`; their raw-input gates, physical execution and manual tie-break remain unchanged. No scene, prefab, serialized tuning, UI, package, settings or Legacy changes.
- **Verified:** normal Unity compilation and `git diff --check` pass. Final focused formal-scene Play Mode regression: **25 PASS / 0 FAIL**, using virtual input and actual executors/physics. Covered both opposite-Move orders and manual fallback; Forward then Back exactly once; both Jump/Dodge and Move/Dodge orders; unavailable Jump/cooling Dodge fallback; commitments surviving newly matching higher rows; parallel Move/Aim/Jump, actual Apex-to-shot and Landed-to-Jump-to-shot chains; six paid Burst shots despite higher Low Ammo Hold Fire, followed by real Burst Finished / Move Finished; committed platform landing; short live Wave 1 and defeat/reconfigure cleanup. Evidence: `Logs/BossEncounter/rule-conflicts-baseline.txt` and `rule-conflicts.txt`. Returned to Edit Mode. Current constraints are mirrored in AGENTS/CLAUDE and ConditionalRules.
- **Limits:** focused controlled fixtures establish arbitration and event/lifecycle behavior, not balance or human playability. ## 2026-10-18 — Three-wave Play Mode hitch diagnosis and optimization

- **Scope / baseline:** Thomas reported Play Mode stutter and requested W1/W2/W3 measurements and performance fixes. Verified Unity 6000.6.0f1, the formal GO GHOST scene and existing uncommitted UI/rule work. No gameplay tuning, UI/art, scene/prefab, package or project-setting changes. The existing development runner now has an opt-in `WavePerformanceSmoke.cs` partial; runtime content does not reference it.
- **Measured cause / implementation:** raw CPU profiles reproduced repeated synchronous Boss planning and transient physics-query arrays. `BossTactics.cs` reuses exact fixed-step public prediction prefixes, identical destination routes and launch-node costs only inside a synchronous planning call. `ArenaNavigation2D.cs` reuses growable query buffers and graph scratch arrays; query filters, traversal order, physical/one-way collision and path costs remain intact. `SwordCombat.cs` / `BossActionExecutor.Sword.cs` reuse the same travel result through one synchronous selection/validation/first-beat decision, discarding it before later decisions. `Projectile.TerrainHit` and ally/Draw queries use growable buffers; projectile hit/reflection executors are unchanged. `ConditionalController.cs` prunes consumed events with one reused live-ID set/predicate instead of nested per-row closures. Small named CPU markers remain for future diagnosis.
- **Matched sampling:** baseline and final each used 20 seconds per wave after two seconds of warmup at 2560x1440, five explicit rules, the same virtual movement/fire pattern, real physics/weapons and live Boss policies. Only temporary actor-instance HP was raised to 10000 to keep the sample alive; no assets or session tuning were changed. This is a comparable workload, not a deterministic replay or a full-fight/balance test. Profiler reading ran outside PlayerLoop, averaging .155/.154/.159 ms per recorded frame in the final capture. Screenshots were taken after timing stopped.
- **Results (W1 / W2 / W3):** maximum summed script-callback time fell from **66.612 / 43.203 / 32.655 ms** to **23.929 / 15.598 / 24.668 ms**. Script frames above 16.67 ms fell from **28 / 7 / 14** to **1 / 0 / 2** per 20-second sample. PlayerLoop allocations fell from **7707 / 5465 / 6638 bytes per frame** to **1943 / 2266 / 2902** (about 75% / 59% / 56% lower). Final PlayerLoop maxima were **24.644 / 27.032 / 25.436 ms**, with zero samples above 33.33 ms in all three waves. Whole Editor frame >16.67 ms counts were 9 / 7 / 13; W3 still contained one 76.725 ms frame, dominated by a 74.522 ms opaque EditorLoop sample. Baseline maximum whole-frame times include Editor/tool interruptions and are not used as an optimization claim.
- **Compile / mechanisms / core flow:** normal Unity compilation PASS and final Console Error/Exception/Assert query empty. `wave-performance-mechanisms.txt`: **12 PASS / 0 FAIL**, covering exact support/sweep/terrain-query results, zero managed bytes for 400 warmed navigation/sight calls, prediction equality and changed-fact invalidation, non-empty ordinary/Camo candidate equivalence (8 / 5 candidates), six-support route equality and changed-origin invalidation, same sword travel with three searches reduced to one, independent real Jump event consumption, AND re-entry and expired-event pruning. A first Camo fixture had zero candidates in both versions and failed the non-empty-fixture assertion; the final supported-position fixture supersedes it. `wave-performance-final.txt`: **5 PASS / 0 FAIL**, real firing in all waves (62 shots each including warmup), pause freeze and menu cleanup. Reviewed the actual W3 capture. Returned to Edit Mode, clean scene, time scale 1, recording off and original input devices restored.
- **Evidence / remaining limits:** [comparison report](../../Logs/BossEncounter/wave-performance-summary.md), raw `wave-performance-{baseline,diagnostic,optimized-a,optimized-b,final}*` frame/marker CSVs, focused reports and three `wave-performance-final-w*.png` captures under `Logs/BossEncounter/`. ## 2026-10-18 — Sword movement purpose, residual footwork and descending crossing

- **Baseline / authority:** Thomas approved all four follow-ups after the idle/jump diagnosis. A live W2 sample held position for 1.47 s of residual waiting plus .66 s of terminal recovery; another sample had only the ordinary .65 s recovery. Both used the same lowered-blade pose. Travel used a fixed .65 s target horizon and forced jump preference; next-strike usefulness was incomplete. Preserved existing uncommitted rework/UI/developer changes and session tuning.
- **Implemented:** `SwordCombat.cs` compares supported walks/dashes and executable jumps using each route's arrival plus the following strike, rejecting destinations without useful next pressure. Short corrections can walk; direct Pursuit competes against Crossing. Crossing can commit a descending attack before takeoff: .40 s preparation/.18 s lock, frozen landing/side/strike offset, one .24 s airborne descending arc, real collision and a finite exit. Missed/failed destinations receive the existing short exclusion. Tells and public blade paths follow the committed route and actual body motion, never a new target after lock.
- **Residual / recovery:** the Sword executor previews a next intent, revalidates it against fresh facts, and permits one same-support step up to .85 units while residuals remain. ## 2026-10-02 — Fix jumping from the Boss's head

## 2026-10-01 — Failure Result restored, two Boss health phases, health bar colours, five platforms

- **Failure Result (replaces the earlier "defeat goes straight to Main Menu" rule):** both outcomes save one frozen record at resolution and stop on Mission Result; defeat: MISSION FAILED -> CONTINUE -> Main Menu; victory: MISSION SUCCESS -> CONTINUE -> Ending -> CONTINUE -> Main Menu. - **Boss: two independent health phases (TEST 600 + 600, both on the Boss's `Enemy` component in `CombatAttempt.prefab`).** `Enemy` gained an optional second pool: when Phase 1 reaches 0 it does not die and fires no death; it is `AwaitingSecondPhase` and rejects every damage source (guns, pellets, blade, deflected bullets, Drone) while staying active and targetable. `BossFight` retires hostile bullets during that window and calls `Enemy.BeginSecondPhase()` when the Boss reports Phase 2. Only Phase 2 reaching 0 is the Boss death -> victory. Result BOSS HP is the current phase's HP; TIME and ACCURACY cover the whole fight (only the practice door resets them).

Known limit of the unchanged Boss code: the transition begins after a Burst/Sweep in progress finishes, so the immune window is 3 s plus up to that remainder; bullets from that remainder are retired at once.

B = `CyberpsychoBoss`, Boss presentation and `AttemptPresentationVfx`; B hands small patches to A. No Boss gameplay patch from B had arrived when this entry was written.

- **Input release gate (`6263d77`), reproduced then fixed:** after input (re)started or focus returned, any single held key (for example D) kept *all* input cleared until every key was released together. Measured with `perf-input` before the fix: D held across a focus return -> moved 0.06 units in .6 s, 0 shots, no jump. After: 3.60 units, 3 shots, jump works. Now each button that is already down at that moment is ignored only until it is released; movement is never gated; focus loss still clears input.
- **Frame time: NOT REPRODUCED.** `perf-input` in editor Play Mode (Game view 2560x1440, unfocused editor so absolute numbers are not standalone numbers): about 1.1-1.3 ms average, p95 under 1.6 ms, no frame over 25 ms in practice idle, sustained fire, P1 or P2; one 16-21 ms frame per multi-second window in every situation including idle, with a constant ~9 KB/frame editor GC baseline. Disabling VFX and Bloom changed nothing measurable. No hotspot was found, so nothing was "optimised"; two small real items were fixed: the ammo readout allocated a string every frame, and bullets had no Rigidbody interpolation (they stepped at the 50 Hz physics rate, visible on a 360 Hz display). Standalone numbers need a hand-played run: start `GO-GHOST-perf.bat` in the build folder (adds `-ggperf`; `FrameTimeLog` writes one `[Perf]` line every 5 s to `perf.log`).
- **Operations that are blocked by rule, not by a bug:** no firing while reloading; Sniper/Shotgun need a new press and ignore presses during their fire interval; base dash is ground-only; dash/blade/Time Slow cooldowns; nothing after death or outside Practice/Combat.
- **Air Dash (confirmed by Thomas):** leg upgrade (`leg_air_dash`, 10%, either leg, never both; UI hides and the draft rejects the duplicate). Left Shift in the air = one horizontal dash per airtime with the existing dash distance, duration and i-frames; shared cooldown; landing restores the air use but not the cooldown; the practice door restores both. Read only from `DeploymentSnapshot.HasAirDash`; legacy `airDodgeUnlocked` is untouched and unused. Controls line `LEFT SHIFT IN AIR — AIR DASH` in addition to `LEFT SHIFT — DODGE`. `air-dash`: 10 PASS / 0 FAIL (all eight requested rules).
- **UI selected state (no new text):** selected body region is filled white, WEAPON button / equipped choice / open Archive tab are inverted (white fill, black text). Archive tabs moved 20 px away from BACK. `ui-polish`: 4 PASS / 0 FAIL plus screenshots.
- **NOT TESTED:** standalone frame times; LMB held across a focus return (per-button gate path is coded, only the D case was measured); audio by ear; any B Boss change (none delivered).
- **Windows x64 build: PASS** (Succeeded, 0 errors, about 17 s incremental). Output `Builds/Windows-Playtest-AirDash/GO-GHOST.exe` (+ `GO-GHOST-perf.bat`); earlier build folders kept. Started headless (`-batchmode`): reached `[Session] Practice`, `[Perf]` logging works, no exceptions. **NOT TESTED in the standalone:** real keyboard/mouse, focus switching, door, Air Dash, win/lose return, sound, frame times (no input injection or focus stealing while the operator uses the desktop). Build residue discarded.

## 2026-10-01 — Three guns, automatic reload, Arm-Blade, round catalogue

- **Baseline:** checkpoint `6ca5027` (practice flow / endings / audio / VFX) pushed first; build residue (URP runtime list, preloaded Input actions) checked hunk by hunk and discarded. Then `9d254df` (guns) and `3fcd504` (Arm-Blade + catalogue).
- **Guns:** Weapon slot offers Assault Rifle (hold to fire, default on launch), Sniper and Shotgun (one trigger pull per press; Shotgun = six pellets in a fixed 25 degree fan). One `PlayerShooting`, one `Projectile`; TEST values in `LoadoutRules.Weapon` (table in Prototype_Test_Profile). Legacy `basic_gun` keeps its label in old records and runs as the Assault Rifle. Rapid Fire scales each weapon's own interval (x1.75 per arm). Weapon stays 0% Cyberization.
- **Magazines:** finite magazine, unlimited reserve. An empty magazine reloads by itself; nothing fires while reloading; a held LMB resumes only the Assault Rifle. No manual reload, no Reload input action (Thomas's override; the R version was removed before any commit). The practice door ends a running reload and refills. Ammo readout above the player HP bar position in both rooms: `12 / 24` or `RELOADING`.
- **Accuracy:** counts gun projectiles (a Shotgun pull adds six to the denominator). Deflected bullets, blade damage and the Drone never count.
- **Arm-Blade (Left Arm, E):** one .35 s swing along the existing aim output, 1 s start interval, box 1.7 x 1.7 in front of the officer. Damages an enemy once per swing (TEST 20). Hostile bullets inside the swing are really deflected: `Projectile.Deflect` reverses the same bullet, hands owner/team to the officer, drops the hostile clock and accuracy owner, keeps the remaining lifetime; no homing or damage bonus. Any hostile bullet can be deflected. Normal Boss blade contacts stay parryable through the existing `TryCounter`; BLACKOUT and Predator Drop are untouched. Legacy Counter Arm keeps its old cancel-on-contact behaviour for older builds only.
- **Catalogue:** Upgrade lists Human plus Left Arm: Arm-Blade / unlocked Drone; Right Arm: Rapid Fire; legs: Double Jump / Hover (never the same ability on both legs; UI hides and the draft rejects the duplicate); Torso: Time Slow; Brain: Targeting AI. - **UI:** three weapon choices, Human choice per body slot, `E — SLASH / DEFLECT` controls line, ammo readout. Case Closure lists only formal HUMAN / AI endings (older TEMP IDs stay in the save, unlisted). No Cyberization display.
- **Feedback reuse (approved clips/VFX only):** one shot cue + muzzle flash per trigger pull; swing = blade cue + blade arc; melee hit = impact cue + impact lines; deflection = counter cue + counter lines. Identical cues in one frame play once. No reload or empty sound exists.
